美時 (1795 TT)  Trading Buy

2026 年毛利率回升，海外 ECB 再融資計畫落地將降低利
息負擔。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優
化趨勢確立。營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升；
(2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集收割期 ， 重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib
預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 EPS 12.08 元，投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利
仍受到併購融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億，毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提
升，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。費用顯著
攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴張所致。  
營業外收支部分， 1H26 非營業費用 25.76 億，其中財務成本達
23.67 億 (YoY+980.82%) ，主要受到美國較高利率環境下產生龐
大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣匯兌損失衝擊。
所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，若排除該項
一次性影響，調整後有效稅率為 22.3% 。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心 ：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注 、 7 月起併入 Sandoz
菲律賓業務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與
Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年
營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率
回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥
出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在 52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將
積極推進債務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、
顯著降低下半年及未來的財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性
並提升資金調度靈活度。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
美時之企業使命「提供全球病患更好、更安全且更負擔得起的藥物」。為追
求使命，同時投入環境與社會之永續發展， 2024 年初，美時已成立「永續
暨風險管理小組」 ，針對公司的永續發展工作建立管理機制，研擬永續發展
相關之短、中、長期目標、策略，並管理監督跨部門之執行過程與成果，以
從系統性的內部管理，極小化美時產品對於社會及環境所帶來的潛在衝擊。
公司簡介：  
美時專精於高門檻的複雜學名藥及癌症用藥的研發、生產與銷售。公司產品
線涵蓋中樞神經系統、癌症、心血管疾病等領域，並在美國、歐洲及亞洲多
國市場擁有廣泛的銷售通路。  
透過策略性併購與全球市場的布局，美時已逐步從區域性藥廠轉型為具備
全球影響力的國際製藥公司。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利仍受到併購
融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億， 1H26 累計營收 174.53 億 (YoY+84.18%) ，創下歷史半
年度新高紀錄。 2Q26 毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提升 3 個百分
點，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。 1H26 營業費用合計
48.22 億 (YoY+93.65%) ，其中管銷費用為 42.96 億 (YoY+97.06%) 、研發費用
為 5.26 億 (YoY+69.68%) ，費用顯著攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴
張所致。  
營業外收支部分 ，1H26 非營業費用合計高達 25.76 億 ， 其中財務成本達23.67
億 (YoY+980.82%) ，其他營業外淨支出為 2.09 億 (YoY-51.40%) ，主要受到美
國較高利率環境下產生龐大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣
匯兌損失衝擊。所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，使本期
實際有效稅率暫時提升至 29.5% ；若排除該項一次性影響，調整後有效稅率
為 22.3% 。  
區域營收佔比與年增：  
市場區域 1H26 佔
比 1H26 金額變動(YoY) 變動說明 
美國 62% +68.97 億(+180%YoY) 併購 Alvogen 業務 
韓國 17% -0.27 億(微幅下滑) 既有成熟產品線自然波動 
B2B 業務 8% +10.42 億(+245%YoY) Nintedanib及 Enzalutamide全
球授權夥伴拉貨爆發 
台灣 7% -0.35 億(微幅下滑) 健保藥價定期調整 
東南亞 5% +0.78 億 區域通路擴展及產品送件核
准放量 
亞洲其他 1% +0.21 億 新興市場授權合作 
1H26 營收在地理分佈上呈現重大轉變，美國市場在 Alvogen 完整合併後營
收大幅增長 180% 達 68.97 億，營收佔比攀升至 62% ，成為公司最核心的營
運市場； B2B 全球外銷業務表現極為亮眼，受惠於 Nintedanib 於歐洲 27 國、
加拿大、台灣與美國上市出貨，以及 Enzalutamide 於加拿大、巴西、以色列

3 群益投顧 
與韓國上市放量， B2B 營收暴增 245% 達 10.42 億，佔比升至 8%；亞洲既有
市場整體保持平穩 (+1%YoY) ，其中東南亞增加 0.78 億、亞洲其他增加 0.21
億，有效抵銷韓國 (-0.27 億 )與台灣 (-0.35 億 )的微幅回檔。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注、 7 月起併入 Sandoz 菲律賓業
務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與 Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產
品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。在毛利
率表現上，隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在
高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在
52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將積極推進債
務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、顯著降低下半年及未來的
財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性並提升資金調度靈活度。  
中長期來看，管線中超過三分之一的專案將於 2026 至 2028 年密集推出，產
品上市能見度極高，將推動公司在 2026 年之後維持成長動能。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優化趨勢確立。
營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升； (2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集
收割期，重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib 預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 
EPS 12.08 元，投資建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

中長期業績展望佳，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年， AI 基礎設施的需求正帶動整體市場
復甦，並外溢至記憶體、網通與電源管理領域，然消費性電子
(如手機、 PC) 終端需求仍偏弱，且庫存天數有微幅上升的跡象。
儘管如此，聯電預期其 2026 年整體晶圓出貨量仍將成長，特別
是在 22/28 nm 與 8 吋的市占率持續提升。聯電預估 2026 年 AI
相關營收將近 3 億美元，並預期 3 年內突破 10 億美元大關。預
估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.72 元。公司在先進封裝 (interposer 或
矽電容等 )、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取得進展，為 2027
年後中長期成長奠定基礎。雖聯電 2Q26 獲利大多以投資收益
為主， 3Q26 股市成長動能相對弱，可能壓抑業外收益，但本業
獲利逐步回升，建議 Buy ，目標價 169 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.36 元：聯電 2Q26 營收為 687.33 億元，
QoQ+12.61% ，整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓 112.90 萬片，
QoQ+10.58% 。 2Q26 產能利用率增加至  85% 。 ASP 穩定向上。
2Q26 產能利用率增至 85% ， ASP QoQ 小增。 2Q26 毛利率增加
3.28 個百分點至 32.48% 。業外認列  302.36 億元收益，包含利
息收益  2.18 億元、匯兌收益 0.03 億元，認列投資收益  300.45 
億元 (1Q26 則為  49.97 億元投資收益 )。聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.36 元 。 若不計算投資收益 ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.97 元，QoQ+9.31% 。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元：預期 3Q26 在電腦、通訊及消費
性領域的需求動能將保持穩定。出貨成長主要由電源管理  IC 、
感測器及微控制器（ MCU ）的強勁需求所推動。 8 吋晶圓產品
線正迎來強勁反彈，預期 3Q26 產能利用率將有顯著改善；而  
12 吋產能目前已處於健康的利用率水位，足以支撐核心業務發
展。預 估 聯 電3Q26 晶圓出貨量 QoQ+7~9% ， 產能利用率約 90%。
ASP in USD QoQ+0% ，不過 2026 年的定價環境更具優勢。這主
要源於市場供需關係的結構性改善，毛利率增至約 35% 左右。
預估聯電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2317 鴻海精密工業(FOXCONN)

報告日期： 2026/09/03         研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收、獲利續創同期新高， EPS 4.27 元 
鴻海 2Q26 營收 2.53 兆， QoQ +19% ，YoY +41% ，創歷年同期
新高。就各產別來看， 2Q26 雲端網路產品佔整體營收的 51% ；
消費智能產品佔 29% ；電腦終端產品佔 15% ；元件及其他產品佔
5%，由於第二季為 ICT 產品的傳統淡季， AI 伺服器營收持續走
高，以至於雲端網路產品比重首次突破五成。而獲利方面， 2Q26
營業毛利 1545.3 億， QoQ +18% ，YoY +36% ，毛利率 6.12% ，
QoQ -0.06ppts ，YoY -0.21ppts ，營業利益 948 億， QoQ +25% ，
YoY+68% ，營利率 3.75% ，QoQ +0.18ppts ，YoY +0.6ppts ，創
歷年單季最高。 2Q26 業外淨利 0.63 億，稅後淨利 599.7 億，
QoQ +20% ，YoY +35% ，單季稅後 EPS 4.27 元，創同期新高。

二、進入下半年傳統旺季、 AI 動能續強， 3Q26 營運有
望持續成長  
公司公告 7 月營收 9465 億， MoM +15.2% ，YoY +54.2% ，今年
累計營收 5.59 兆，年增 37.9% 。展望 3Q26 ，公司表示，受惠於
AI 動能持續成長，再加上 ICT 產品進入下半年的傳統旺季，預期
第三季營收將顯著成長。而就各產品別來看，雲端網路產品方面，
受惠於 AI 需求維持高速成長，且 CSP 客戶持續上調資本支出，
帶動 AI 機櫃持續放量，公司預估季對季與年對年都將呈現強勁成
長，並預估第三季 AI 機櫃出貨量將較第二季呈現高雙位數成長。
消費智能產品部分，則由於進入傳統旺季，預期季對季與年對年
皆將有顯著成長。而電腦終端產品方面，由於新產品需求高峰已
過，再加上記憶體供應緊張，預期季對季將呈現略微衰退。預估
公司 3Q26 營收 2.83 兆， QoQ +12.8% ，YoY +37.7% ，毛利率
6.1% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利 683 億元，單季稅後 EPS 4.92
元。

三、預估 2026 年 EPS 18.63 元 
展望 2026 年，由於 AI 伺服器需求維持強勁需求，訂單能見度提
升，公司維持全年強勁成長的看法不變。 AI 機櫃部分，公司維持
全年 AI 機櫃出貨量倍數以上成長預期，並預計 GB 系列產品需求
將至少延續至明年，整體出貨逐季成長的預期不變。而針對新一
代 AI 機櫃，公司預計第三季開始進行量產準備，第四季開始出貨，
預期在新一代平台的市佔率將進一步提升。 LPU 及 Vera 相關產
投資評等：買進

品目前處於前期量產準備階段，預計將在第三季開始生產，第四
季逐步放量。而網通產品部分， 800G 以上交換機全年營收有望
倍增， CPO 全光交換機也將按計畫在第三季量產出貨。另外，
ASIC 部分，受惠於新客戶的導入以及增加新的 ASIC 專案，預計
今年 ASIC 專案將有顯著成長，且今年上半年 ASIC 營收佔比已略
高於去年全年。而針對今年營利率展望，公司表示，今年上半年
營業利潤率已達 3.67% ，較去年同期增加 0.67 個百分點，公司預
估今年全年將超越去年 3.2% 的表現。至於資本支出方面，為因應
AI 伺服器機櫃及區域製造需求，預期全年資本支出仍將年增 3 成
以上，用於擴充伺服器、機櫃等關鍵產能，並強化全球製造與自
動化佈局。預估 2026 全年營收 10.8 兆， YoY +33.5% ，毛利率
6.1% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利 2588 億，全年稅後 EPS 18.63
元。預估 2027 全年營收 12.4 兆， YoY +15% ，毛利率 5.9% ，營
利率 3.3% ，稅後淨利 3116 億，全年稅後 EPS 22.43 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 335 元 
考量到 CSP 持續上修 2026 年資本支出展望， AI 伺服器維持強勁
需求，公司預期今年 AI 機櫃出貨量將達成倍數成長，且隨著新一
代 AI 機櫃推出，有望進一步拓展公司在 AI 伺服器領域市佔率。
目前公司股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 11~12 倍本益比，評價仍有
提升空間，給予投資建議為買進，目標價 335 元(15x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
鴻海精密工業 (FOXCONN) 為全球最大 eCMMS( 電子化 -零元件、
模組機光電垂直整合服務 )廠，在電子代工服務領域 (EMS) 排名全
球第一，市占率超過四成。鴻海近年積極投入「電動車、數位健
康、機器人」三大新興產業以及「人工智慧、半導體、新世代通
訊技術」三項新技術領域，以「 3+3 」結合作為集團重要的長期
發展策略。在 EMS 板塊，鴻海主要競爭對手為和碩聯合科技、
廣達電腦、緯創資通、仁寶電腦、英業達及立訊精密等。

圖一、鴻海產品營收佔比

資料來源：鴻海、宏遠投顧整理

1. 四大 CSP 上修今年資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在生成式 AI 商機快速擴張帶動下，全球雲端基礎設施投資進入新
一輪高峰。亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和微軟認為其在先前的鉅額投資已
帶動營收明顯成長，同時預估未來 AI 潛在商機仍持續擴大。其中，
亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 已陸續上調 2026 全年投資金額，且
四大雲端服務供應商 (CSP) 總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新
高規模。亞馬遜、微軟、 Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分
別為 1,306 億、 1,701 億美元，同時亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 再
上修 2026 全年資本支出金額， 2026 年資本支出預估將達 7,450
億美元。而根據 TrendForce 最新產業研究，全球主要雲端服務
供應商 (CSP) 為加速建置 AI 基礎設施，資本支出預估於 2026 年
將年增 98% ，2027 年再成長 50% 至 1.3 兆美元左右，年增率收
斂的主要原因，除了反映高基期因素之外，部分可歸咎為鉅額資

本支出對四大 CSP 財務衝擊日益擴大。然而，在 AI Inference 、
Agentic AI 、自研 ASIC 和新一代 AI 模型持續推升算力需求的情
況下，預期整體 CSP 投資金額將續創新高紀錄，產業成長有望從
原先以 GPU 擴張為主，轉為包含 AI server 、液冷散熱、先進封
裝、高速互聯、電源和記憶體等基礎設施全面升級的態勢。

圖二、 2024-2027 年全球九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估 (單位 :十億美元 )

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31% 。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應
對生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中，
Google 2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出
貨量有望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為
GPU AI server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量
能將再擴大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、 AMD 
Helios 整櫃式方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，
為該公司長期降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

2606 裕民航運 ( U-MING )

報告日期： 2026 / 09 / 10 研 究 員：林奕頎（ Yichi Lin ） 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.預期  202 6 年 稅後 EPS + 8.22 元， YoY + 91%，創  18 年新高  
2024 年全年  BDI 平均  1,755  ； 2025 年全年  BDI 平均  1,681 。 
2026 年（截至 9 月 8 日）  BDI 平均  2,5 12： 
1Q / 2026 單季 BDI 平均  1,955； 2Q / 2026 單季 BDI 平均  2,751。 
3Q / 2026 單季（截至 9 月 8 日） BDI 平均  2,9 28。 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI 3,5 84。 運價 強勢，推升航商獲利 。 
二.美伊衝 突日益激烈 ，荷姆斯海峽封閉，油輪運價快速上漲  
裕民 船隊有  3 艘原油油輪  及 1 艘成品油輪 。  美伊 停戰 破局 ： 
原油油輪 運價指數：從 7 月 2 日 1,850 ，漲至 9 月 8 日 2,938 ，
累計漲幅  59%  ； 成品油輪 運價指數：從 7 月 7 日 995，漲至
9 月 8 日 1,702，累計漲幅  71%。 油輪運價 上漲，裕民將受惠。  
三.預期 4Q 是 2026 年單季獲利高峰 ， 稅後EPS + 2.41 元，YoY + 55%  
(1) 美伊衝突， 荷姆斯海峽 封閉 ，天然氣供給中斷 ； 4Q 取暖
旺季，各國 將被迫 以燃煤取代天然氣，推升海運燃煤需求 。 
(2) 聯合國預期 超級聖嬰現象至少將持續到 2027 年 2 月，極端
氣候橫行 ； 巴拿馬運河缺水 嚴峻，持續降低船舶通行量，
將壓抑海運供給 。  即使超級聖嬰解除；  依過去 5 年資料
（參閱圖 1）， 巴拿馬運河季節性乾季最低水位會落在 5 月。 
(3) 散裝航運以  4Q 為例行的季節性 旺季 。 
基於 (1) ~ (3) ，宏遠預期 4Q 將是裕民  2026 年單季獲利高峰。  
四.基於保守，估 2027 年 BDI 僅 2,490 ，裕民稅後 EPS + 7.10 元 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI 指數  3,5 84。 
宏遠 基於保守 原則，假設  三大利空在 2027 年落實 ： 
(1) 荷姆斯 海峽不再受阻 ，天然氣供給上升，壓抑煤炭需求 。 
(2) 超級 聖嬰解 除，巴拿馬運河不再缺水。  
(3) 全球散裝 船舶 下水量大增 。 
宏遠 估 2027 年 BDI 平均值僅  2,490； 裕民稅後 EPS + 7.10 元。 
若上述三項利空沒有落實，則必須調高裕民  2027 年獲利預估 。 
五.建議： 4Q 旺季  遇上  戰爭及聖嬰；  估 2026 年獲利，18 年新高  
2026 年截至 9 月 8 日，BDI 指數都 大幅 高於 2025 年同期（參閱
圖 2）； 此外，美伊戰爭造成 荷姆斯海峽中斷 ，須以燃煤取代
天然氣，推升散裝海運需求；  而 超級聖嬰 帶來全球乾旱，即
將撞上  巴拿馬運河  每年 12 月 ~ 隔年 5 月的 季節性 乾季（參
閱圖 1），巴拿馬運河缺水將更加嚴重；  宏遠 估裕民 2026 年稅
後 EPS  + 8.22 元，YoY + 91%，創 18 年新高 ； 持續 建議買進 。 
投資評等：買進

參考價位：79.7 元

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
截至 2026 年 5 月（法說資料），裕民實際經營船隻為 71 艘：  
海岬型船   25 艘：自有 22 艘 + 3 艘合資。  
巴拿馬型船 24 艘：自有 14 艘 + 7 艘代管  + 3 艘合資 。 
輕便型      8 艘（自有） 。 
水泥船      4 艘（自有） 。 
油輪        4 艘：透過  環能海運 （持股 40% ）。 
CTV 離岸風電人員運輸船 6 艘（自有  ； 裕民風能航運 ）。

在建船隻  12 艘，交船時程 ： 
202 6 年： 輕便型  1 艘（預期 11 月交付）。 
202 7 年 及 2028 年： 
海岬型  4 艘 + 輕便型  5 艘 + 風電工程船  2 艘。

二.營運概況 及展望  
1.超級聖嬰 造成缺水  撞上  巴拿馬運河乾季，壓抑海運供給  
2026 年 5 月，全球進入聖嬰年，加劇全球乾旱與極端高溫 的
氣候異常 。 聯合國  世界氣象組織（ WMO ）表示， 這波聖嬰
現象，可能是過去  40 年最強一次，幾乎肯定會持續到  2027
年 2 月，很可能打破  2023 年 ~ 2024 年的紀錄 。

巴拿馬運河溝通大西洋與太平洋，節省海運船舶至少  3,000
海哩的航程，航行時間縮短  2 週左右。

但由於 大西洋與太平洋的海平面和水流高度不同，船隻 要透
過 巴 拿 馬 運 河 ，橫 跨 美 洲 ， 必 須依 靠 巴 拿 馬 運 河 的船閘 注
水，採取類似 爬樓梯一樣 的升降 動作 ，讓船 隻從海平面被提
升到高處的加通湖，穿過湖面後，再降回另一側的海平面。
每艘船舶 每次通過船閘，都需要消耗  加通湖  數以百萬加侖
計算的淡水。

因此， 加通湖的水量多寡 ，會影響巴拿馬運河的運作效率與
容量 ，一旦缺水，巴拿馬運河每日通行船舶數量就會減少。

觀察過去 5 年（2021 年 ~ 2025 年）加通湖的水位資料（參閱
圖 1） ，通常會從每年 5 月，逐月爬升到  12 月；  但 2026 年
超級聖嬰的乾旱，造成目前 9 月水位不升反降 。 
以現貨為主，長約為輔 
總船隊 71 艘 
全球 超級聖嬰 極端氣候 
從 2026 年 5 月 至少持續 
至 2027 年 2 月

巴拿美運河 季節性乾季 從 
每年 12 月 持續至 隔年 5 月

聖嬰 + 乾季 
預期巴拿馬運河 要解除缺水 
最快必須等到 2027 年 2H

2026 年 5 月起，全球 超級聖嬰 ，帶來的 乾旱，至少將持續到
2027 年 2 月 ； 如此，將撞上  加通湖  每年  例行 的 季節 性 
乾季 （每年 12 月 ~ 隔年 5 月 ； 參閱圖 1 ）。

因此， 巴拿馬運河（ 加通湖）的缺水很可能會持續加重，最
快的解除時間可能要等到  2027 年 2H。

巴拿馬運河  每日正常通行量  36 ~ 40 艘。 
2023 年 ~ 2024 年 全球 聖嬰 造成乾旱  巴拿馬運河的 每日船
舶通行量最低降至  18 艘。

目前  加通湖  尚未進入  季節性乾季，但 受制於全球聖嬰現
象， 加通湖  水位  已經 提前 明顯降低 ； 巴拿馬運河管理當
局， 已經 宣布，巴拿馬運河每日船舶通行數量， 2026 年 9 月
3 日降至  34 艘 ； 9 月 15 日降至  32 艘。

宏遠預期， 巴拿馬運河降低每日船舶通行數量的趨勢，將日
益嚴重， 壓抑所有的海運供給 ，將有利於推升各型船舶的運
價，拉高海運航商獲利 。

圖 1：加通湖 過去 5 年 每月平均水位變化 ； 5 月 走升至 12 月（雨季） ； 12 月 再走跌至 5 月（乾季） 
超級聖嬰帶來乾旱，造成 2026 年 9 月水位 不升反降 。

單位：英尺。 
資料來源： 網路公開資訊  ； 宏遠  。

2.散裝 BDI 走勢，隱示  2026 年獲利  將大幅優於  2025 年 
2026 年截至 9 月 8 日，散裝 BDI 運價走勢都優於 2025 年同期
表現（ 參閱圖 2） 。 
受惠於 BDI 的強勁走勢 ，裕民 ： 
1Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 1.17 元，優於  1Q / 2025 同期  + 0.27 元。 
2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，優於  2Q / 2025 同期  + 0.77 元。

從 BDI 的表現來看：  
1Q / 2026 （過年淡季） BDI 平均  1,955 。 
2Q / 2026 （美伊戰爭爆發， 燃煤替代天然氣） BDI 平均  2,751 。 
3Q / 2026 （截至 9 月 8 日） BDI 平均  2,928。 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI  3,584 。

4Q 是散裝 航運的 季節性 旺季，遇上， 荷姆斯海峽封閉  推升  
煤 炭 的 海 運 需 求  及   巴 拿 馬 運 河 缺 水 降低  海運船舶供
給（繞道等同減少供給） ； 預期 4Q 的 BDI 將會持續強勁。

BDI 漲勢 強勁，宏遠預期 裕民  3Q ~ 4Q / 2026 單季獲利 YoY 大
幅成長 ； 未來， 3Q 及 4Q 財報發布 ，將激勵股價表現 。

### Page 1

M
Idea
Greater China Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
Memory Innovation –  The Focus
from SEMICON Taiwan
SEMICON Taiwan highlighted memory value creation shifting
from bit scaling toward architecture-level innovation. OW on AP
Memory for emerging 3D WoW stacking. OW on Montage for
memory interface opportunities. OW on Macronix for SLC
NAND.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
For a broader discussion of these technologies, please refer to our Global Insight:
Innovating the Next-Generation Memory.
Memory is becoming a system-level differentiator: AI performance is increasingly
constrained by memory rather than computing. Longer context, agentic workflows,
and KV cache growth are raising the importance of bandwidth, capacity, and latency,
with tokens/$ and tokens/W becoming more relevant metrics. We see this

reinforcing the strategic importance of memory architecture as AI inference scales.
3D stacking is likely to be the future trend beyond today's 2.5D architecture:.
Samsung's zHBM and broader DRAM-on-logic concepts point to memory moving
from beside computinge toward vertically integrated architecture. This should
increase the importance of hybrid bonding, WoW integration, and thermal co-design.
We see AP Memory as a potential beneficiary of emerging 3D WoW stacking

opportunities.
Memory interfaces are becoming more strategic: As AI systems adopt
heterogeneous memory tiers across HBM, DRAM, CXL, SRAM and flash,
connectivity and control between computing and memory become complex. Google
mentioned that CXL is used to leverage old DDR4. We believe this raises the value
of memory interface solutions and reinforces our OW view on Montage.
(Continued in the following section)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Architecture-level innovations span memory interfaces 3D integration,
computing-near-memory, and new AI use cases on NAND.
Inference shifts Al bottleneck to memory bandwidth, capacity, and data
movement.
3D stacking is likely to be the next major architecture beyond today's 2.5D HBM,
supporting new opportunities for Top Pick AP Memory.

NAND moves up the Al memory hierarchy; creates new opportunities beyond
storage.
Memory and computing are converging through PIM, custom HBM, and near-
memory architectures.
September 1, 2026 06:03 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Overview (cont'd)
NAND is gaining a more active role in Al systems: Fast NAND, high-IOPS SSDs, and HBF
are moving closer to computing for KV cache, model weights, and inference workloads,
broadening NAND's role beyond storage. We see this as supportive for higher-performance
NAND and potential incremental SLC NAND demand, reinforcing our OW view on
Macronix.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the industry's cyclical
nature. We apply a target multiple of 2.1x to our 2027 BVPS estimate vs. its historical average
of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as the MLC and legacy
TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under-supply easily reaching 40%. We

believe the only supplier that could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles
AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)

Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost
of equity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) medium-term
growth rate of 16.0%, 3) payout ratio of 67%, 4) terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected consumer demand
n Faster-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Milder competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other

design houses
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected consumer demand
n Slower-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Intensifying competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Montage Technology Co Ltd (6809.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n 8.4% CoE (1.2 beta, 3.0% risk-free rate, and 4.5% risk premium)
n 30% payout ratio
n 19.3% medium-term growth rate
n 4% terminal growth rate

These assumptions reflect cloud capex growth, DRAM interface technology migration, and
Montage's strong position in China's datacenter semi localization. We then apply an exchange

### Page 6

Idea

rate of 1.15 HKD:1 RMB, assuming no H-share discount vs the A-share.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected phase-out of US peers
n Faster-than-expected spec migration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected DRAM interface technology migration
n Delay in new product launches

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

2 September 2026

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

Unimicron
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Unimicron's ability to generate stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a market risk
premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target
price is equivalent to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus PE multiple of the past five

years. However, as the market focuses on 2027 upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI
business; 2) significant increase in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure from
depreciation due to higher capex investments.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

For the month of September 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will increase 
to Rmb827mn (+6% MoM, +17% YoY)/NT$3,930mn (+6% MoM, +27% YoY) 
We expect September revenue will go up by 6% MoM to Rmb827mn/NT$3,930mn, 
mainly driven by the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb30.9mn in Sep vs. 
Rmb 29.1mn in Aug) despite the same number of working days. The improvement in 
daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key customers, particularly 
in electronics (we also expect peers to see strong demand in electronics, see here), 
followed by batteries and machine tools, and we expect demand to improve following 
the inventory pull-in after the Europe summer holiday.

Investment view: Strong YoY momentum in electronics could drive overall growth. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we continue to see key end-segments 
performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 20%+ YoY

strong YTD performance is in line with our expectation that AirTAC is a key beneficiary 
of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in mainland China, as 
well as benefiting from the prolonged upcycle supported by government stimulus 
policies. However, we maintain caution on the potential of exaggerated orders (including 
advance or duplicate orders), and will continue to monitor the underlying end-demand 
closely. 
Furthermore, we believe the company is well-positioned to continue to gain market 
share in the China pneumatic market, particular given its pricing strategy, (1) its 
pneumatic products pricing remain competitively priced at 10%+ below international 
peers, and (2) the company has maintained stable pricing in China (China accounts for 
~95% of total revenue in 2026E per GSe) amid the inflationary cost environment, while 
the company will continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the 
rising cost risk (see here). As a result, we continue to see backlog accumulating and 
expect the company to maintain a high UTR to digest these orders. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
On the latest earnings call, Broadcom's CEO noted that the company is building
substrate capacity in its Singapore factory with a partner. We believe he was
referring to AST, the JV Broadcom has been developing with Toppan (7911.T, Not
Covered) since March 2024 (news). This is Toppan's first overseas substrate plant,
with phase 1 of the two-phase fab targeted to start production by EoY 2026. We see

Broadcom securing FC-BGA substrate supply rather than bringing production in-
house, and therefore expect limited impact on the current supply positions of
Unimicron and NYPCB.
On BT substrate pricing, the price hikes since 2H25 have been driven by raw material
cost inflation, including T glass, gold and copper. We have expected T glass supply
constraints to peak in 2026 and gradually ease from 2027 as more suppliers are
qualified. Thus, it would not surprise us if raw-material-driven price hikes plateau

going forward. That said, our OW thesis on Unimicron and NYPCB centers on ABF
substrate tightness, so we see limited impact from BT price fluctuations on our
ratings for both stocks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
NYPCB's share price was down 12% in the past two trading days (vs. TAIEX up
0.8%).
Investors have asked us if NYPCB would face share losses if Broadcom (AVGO,
covered by Joe Moore) were to produce substrates on its own.
Separately, we have been receiving questions from investors about the pricing
outlook for BT substrate in 4Q.

We see limited impact on our OW thesis for Unimicron and NYPCB, both of which
produce BT substrates and are Broadcom's incumbent suppliers.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

the next 12-18 months.

### Page 1

Ethan Jia
Research Associate
Ethan.Jia@morganstanley.com                                                +852 3963-2287
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
AI semi capex spending in Taiwan remains strong into 2027 to
solve the supply chain bottleneck. Keep OW on TSMC,
MediaTek, Hon Precision, Allring, MPI, Winway, etc.
Titans of Taiwan AI semi supply chain - key message from the  CEO Forum
fireside chat  –  Taiwan is still one of the bottlenecks of AI infrastructure buildout.

On the stage, TSMC Deputy Co-COO Mr. Hou  said that he had never seen demand
growing and changing this frequently as it has over the past six months, while the
tool delivery from WFE partners are still not enough.  MediaTek CEO Mr. Tsai was
asking for more capacity support from ASE, TSMC, and Unimicron.
Why investors came to this trade show in Taiwan: We believe the tech industry
and capital markets are finding ways to keep AI hardware spending sustainable in

coming years. With memory becoming the major bottleneck and cost item for AI
capex, investors are checking whether technologies can improve AI computing
efficiency, for example, CPO to replace copper connectors in XPU scale-up. All in all,
the goal is to reduce per-token cost.  Please see following section for details.
Key focus areas during  SEMICON Taiwan:
1. Silicon photonics for AI networking and  testing process development : In
our previous report, AI Supply Chain: GPU Architecture Optimization Given

Memory Shortage and Supernode Challenge, we indicated that future GPU/
ASIC scale-up would depend on CPO/NPO (“inter-rack CPO connectivity
remains Nvidia's preferred direction”). For example, the NV576 Rubin Ultra in
2027 may use CPO switch trays to connect eight NVL72 server racks. Silicon
Photonics and PIC (Photonic integrated circuit), lasers, CPO, and related
testing present large investment opportunities.
2. Memory optimization from high cost : Instead of adding density, increasing

bandwidth and fully utilizing existing memory resources has become the key
technology trend. Our recent supply chain checks suggest that Rubin Ultra
CPU is highly likely to use 8-Hi HBM4, around one-third fewer than 12-Hi in
Rubin. But by increasing HBM4 base-die units and migrating to HBM4e, plus
some software optimization, total memory bandwidth should be sufficient
for a token output increase.
3. "More than Moore"- Panel-level packaging and Intel EMIB-T - We visited the

Toray (Toray Industries covered by Takato Watabe) booth to check EMIB-T
substrate manufacturing yield, because the company supplies the TCB
bonder.  We expect Intel to provide technical support, and expect MediaTek’s
TPU v9 (HumuFish) to get sufficient EMIB-T substrate supply in 2028.
4. Although it wasn’t the main focus of the SEMICON , we think TSMC's
Moore’s Law migration (for energy efficiency) and MediaTek's ASIC enabling
(to save on chip budgets) are  keys to make AI capex more sustainable.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 6, 2026 05:24 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Need for more capacity to support strong AI semi demand
into 2027
Titans of Taiwan AI semi supply chain - CEO Forum
The discussion focused on how strong AI demand is leading to ongoing undersupply from
the whole semi industry . We believe AI demand remains strong and is able to support
strong revenue growth for ASE, TSMC, and MediaTek in coming years. At the same time,
Taiwan's semi supply chain is also expanding capex and opex aggressively to catch up to

the demand.
Stock ideas around SEMICON Taiwan:We remain OW on TSMC given its dominance in
leading-edge foundry. MediaTek remains our Top Pick given its competitive ASIC design
service. We hosted a booth tour to visit Hon Precision, AllRing, MPI, and WinWay to cross-
check CoWoS and CoPoS expansion plans, as well as optical insertion technology. We are
OW on all the above stocks.
Key focus areas during SEMICON Taiwan 2026
1. Silicon Photonics for AI networking and the testing process development:

Please also see Semiconductor Production Equipment: SEMICON Taiwan Day 1 –
Takeaways from SiPh Seminar from Suzune Tamura. We share the view with Tamura-san
that testing is the key challenge for CPO  mass production. During SEMICON Taiwan,
several companies showcased their solutions or components  for CPO testing. Although
the solutions might be different, all companies were providing similar information: that
CPO testing is still not clearly defined while most of the companies are still trying to get

qualified by major customers with their equipment/solutions. The speed and precision of
optical alignment remains the key to throughput, and  we believe there is still room for
improvement.

2. Memory optimization:  Meanwhile, memory stacking on logic can improve bandwidth.

support, and expect MediaTek’s TPU v9 (HumuFish) to get sufficient EMIB-T substrate
supply in 2028. For 3D IC packaging, please also see Semiconductor Production
Equipment: SEMICON Taiwan Day 2 - Heterogeneous Integration Seminar Takeaways.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

ramp-up of D2C satellites from 2H27, with PCB area consumption to further increase by

that is similar to LEO satellites, but at a significantly larger annual deployment volume of

products, except for SLP products for the key smartphone customer.

business, including (1) strong demand for high-end PCB for AI satellites from its key LEO
customers (as mentioned in bullet point 4), (2) PCB for AI servers from the key
smartphone customer, which is expected to ramp-up from 4Q26, (3) additional
contributions from China customers with PCB demand across switches and AI servers,
and (4) an ongoing project with a small-scale ASIC client.
PCB/CCL sector read-across
We retain our positive view on the optical transceivers’ mSAP demand outlook, while our

supply chain checks recently suggested mSAP as a key bottleneck for the 1.6T optical
transceivers supply chain now, whose product pricing has gone up by 30%+ YTD with
good profitability (our checks and Compeq’s comments suggest the GM for 1.6T optical
transceivers mSAP are generally 40-45%+). We believe ZDT has the best opportunity to
gain more market share in this high-growth and profitable market (we expect TAM for
1.6T+ optical transceivers mSAP to go up from US$0.8bn in 2026 to US$2.9bn in 2028,

implying 94% CAGR), considering it has the most aggressive mSAP capacity expansion
plan (we expect ZDT to expand mSAP capacity by 5x in the next two years) and the
company’s best-in-class BT/mSAP production technology, which could become the
company’s key revenue driver (we expect mSAP to account for ~8%/10%+ of ZDT
revenue in 2027/28 from ~2% in 2026) and margin driver (mSAP GM should be 40-45%+
vs. ZDT 1H26 GM at only ~22+%).
We also expect CCL suppliers, such as EMC and TUC, to benefit from the strong optical

transceiver demand, and we expect both companies to gain more share with more CCL
capacity to be ready in 2027/28 (see here and here).
Overall, we retain our positive view on ZDT’s business outlook not only on mSAP
products, but also seeing a significant margin expansion opportunity from the high-end
AI PCB and ABF substrate products, where we also see a strong demand outlook (see our
AI PCB industry report here and our ABF substrate industry report here).

We maintain our Buy ratings on ZDT, EMC and TUC with unchanged 12m TPs of
NT$925/NT$10,200/NT$3,010.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$925 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line with industry downcycle 
average valuation).  
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market. 
Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Update
Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +1% MoM/+42%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Hiwin's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$2,708mn (+1% MoM/+42% YoY).
• QTD revenue was 67% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$7,996mn (+6% QoQ/
+33% YoY), and 68% of consensus, at NT$7,884mn (+5% QoQ/+32% YoY).
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 is tracking slightly ahead of expectations.

• Looking into 2H26, we maintain the view that Hiwin should continue to see
revenue growth amid the industry recovery and margin expansions driven by
higher utilization/price hikes.
• We stay constructive on the stock, with current valuation at 29x 2027e P/E
vs. a peak cycle level of 35-40x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +1% MoM/+344%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on King Slide's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price movement.
Details:
• August sales were NT$6,466mn (+1% MoM/+344% YoY) – another record
high.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of MSe, at NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348%
YoY), and 77% of consensus, at NT$16,755mn (+55% QoQ/+284% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 revenue tracking in line with our estimate.

• On top of the mainstream GPU/ASIC server demand from hyperscalers, we
believe that King Slide is generating more revenue from various projects in
different applications as well.
• We believe the company should continue to benefit from server market
growth and increasing dollar content, driven by more complex designs and
broader adoption of rail kits. We view it as a good proxy for the AI capex
cycle, given its dominant position, strong patent portfolio, and solid

execution.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Idea
Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
iPhone 18: Real Upgrade Cycle,
but Select Optical Valuations
Look Stretched
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate
Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific

Industry View In-Line
Exhibit                                                                              1                        :                    Summary of changes
Share prices as at: Sep 7, 2026.  Source: FactSet, Morgan Stanley Research
estimates.
We expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to be the key
beneficiaries of the new iPhone launch. We downgrade Largan
to EW and Genius to UW.  Both can benefit from the variable
aperture lens upgrade, but enthusiasm over Co-Packaged Optics

has made the stocks overvalued.
The iPhone opportunity is real for Largan and Genius…but their stocks have
rallied 48% and 85%, respectively, in the past month: (The TAIEX is up 5% over
this period.)
We now value Largan at NT$7,100/share – no longer attractive: Our analysis
suggests that Largan's current share price already embeds more than 30% global
market share in Fiber Array Units (FAU) before formal production starts in 2H27 or
even 2028. Our residual income model yields a value of NT$3,000/share for the

company's traditional business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$4,100/
share for CPO/FAU new business, bringing the total price target to NT$7,100.
However, any delay in CPO/FAU development poses significant downside risk. We
therefore downgrade to EW.
We now value Genius at NT$910/share – but  even on this basis, we view it as
overvalued: Similarly, we believe Genius's share price is also inflated by hopes for
FAU components. Using a similar approach, we conclude that aggregate value is NT

$910/share (NT$600 for existing business and NT$310/share for FAU component
business). We recommend taking profits.
Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal and AAC: We believe all four companies
will benefit from the new iPhone launch. Here, however,  we still see upside in the
stocks. We see these companies, benefiting in various ways:
• Luxshare: Key player in iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max assembly, also benefits from
variable aperture upgrade as component supplier.

•  Lingyi: Higher content value form both iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and foldable.
•  Crystal: Micro prism modules, coated filters, and cover lenses should enjoy
positive ASP and volume growth.
• AAC: Vapor chamber (VC) heat dissipation upgrade for iPhone 18 Pro Max
and Pro is the key catalyst.
Remain EW on Sunny Optical and GoerTek: Sunny is a key variable aperture
component vendor, but it's been well on track to meet the goal of doubling revenue,
implying limited upside surprise. GoerTek could face labor shortages and margin

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 10, 2026 08:01 AM GMT

Executive Summary
With Largan and Genius's significant outperformance YTD, we believe the iPhone 18
launch is less likely to become a key driver of further stock price movement for them.
Instead, our analysis suggests that the CPO enthusiasm could have already made these
stocks overvalued. We therefore downgrade Largan from OW to  EW and downgrade
Genius from EW  to UW.
Meanwhile, we expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to become the key beneficiaries

amid iPhone new product launches. These stocks haven't rallied materially, and we see
upside potential in them.
We maintain EW ratings on Sunny and GoerTek and expect limited positive surprise from
the iPhone peak season.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Implications from iPhone 18 Launch for Various Supply
Chain Companies
Camera spec upgrade is a positive earnings driver, but CPO
enthusiasm has made Largan and Genius overvalued
The iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max have applied a variable aperture main camera for the first
time, representing a significant hardware upgrade.  The ASP of  variable aperture lenses will
be higher than it is for traditional lenses. Thus, a rapid shipment increase in 3Q26 could

become a key re-rating catalyst – indeed, it has already prompted sharp re-rating for
Largan, Genius, and Sunny.
Besides this, the launch of Apple's first foldable iPhone will also provide an incremental
demand driver beyond the standard iPhone 18 lineup. According to Nikkei Asia (link),
foldable smartphone orders increased from 8 million units to 10 million units this year,
and if the new product performs well in 4Q2026, it could become a positive catalyst for

Apple supply chain companies.
Beyond variable aperture and foldable, the further development of a new periscope lens
with the key customer is likely to expand  these use of periscope lenses in the future. This
could also become a positive driver for optical lens companies in the supply chain.
1. Largan: iPhone opportunity is real, but recent enthusiasm
over CPO made valuation too aggressive – downgrade to EW
Largan's revenue follows a well-established seasonal pattern tied to Apple's annual iPhone

launch cycle. After shipments declined continuously from March 2026 through June 2026,
we expect a seasonal recovery to begin in 3Q26, with sequential increases highly likely in
the following months. Largan's August 2026 consolidated revenue reached NT$5.012
billion, up 3% month-over-month and its best performance in four months, and the
company expects September's shipment momentum to surpass August's.
Largan is a key supplier of variable aperture lenses for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and the

company could also benefit from foldable and periscope opportunities, we raise our
intrinsic value estimate of the company's traditional business from NT$2,814/share to NT
$3,000/share. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 18.
Besides the Apple lens opportunity, we believe Largan's share performance is also highly
correlated to CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO
opportunity, we undertook the following analysis:

1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
7.78mn in 2027,with a further surge to 48.6mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU (Fiber
Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple optical
fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics) volume for
CPO also grows from 0.39 to 48.6 mn for the same period of time;
2. We assume ASP of US$100 in 2026-2028 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
2027 and 40% in 2028 (previously 20%).
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$4mn in 2027 and US$579 mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, and
considering the first year of scalable production), it could bring incremental market cap of
US$20.278bn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% rate, we derive a per-share positive
impact of NT$4,100.

We hold a positive view on Largan's CPO/FAU business opportunity because the company
has a superior track record of high precision manufacturing relative to most industry peers
in Taiwan. However, our calculation above suggests that the current share price already
reflects Largan's potential  to attain >30% global market share for FAU products.
Meanwhile, formal production hasn't kicked off yet – it is more likely to start in the

second half of 2027 or even 2028. Therefore, any delay in CPO/FAU development could
result in significant share price fluctuation. Considering current risk-reward, even after
raising our price target 47%, we no longer view Largan as attractive any more in view of
the recent strong share price performance. We set our price target at NT$7,100, within
which NT$3,000 is from its traditional business and NT$4,100 is from CPO/FAU new
business.
2. Genius: Thesis is similar to Largan's –  CPO opportunities

also overpriced, downgrade to UW
Genius, as the second-largest optical lens vendor to Apple, is also well positioned to
benefit from the camera spec upgrade highlighted in the previous section. Considering the
growth opportunities discussed above, we raise our intrinsic value estimate for the

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
existing business from NT$425 to NT$600. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 17.
Similar to Largan, we believe Genius' share price performance is also highly correlated to
CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO opportunity,
we undertook the following analysis:
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall

solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and
30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$54mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple vs. Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US

$1,322mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$310.
